TL;DR: 本文研究了外延石墨烯/SiC体系中Au插层诱导的团簇相，解释了实验中观测到的M点附近准粒子干涉（QPI）椭圆结构及其驻波图案。通过T矩阵方法，证明Au插层于石墨烯空位（hollow site）下方会在周围六个碳原子上产生局域散射势，从而形成环形散射体，产生与实验FT-STS测量一致的QPI特征，并自然生成准静态驻波。此外，计算了团簇内外局域态密度对比，其符号和幅度强烈依赖能量，与实验观测吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上在SiC外延石墨烯中Au插层产生了团簇相，观测到M点附近异常的驻波和准粒子干涉特征，但其微观起源尚不清楚，需要理论解释来理解该现象的物理机制。

Method: 采用T矩阵处理方法，将Au插层于石墨烯hollow site下方视为在周围六个碳原子上产生局域散射势的环形散射体，据此计算QPI结构和实空间驻波图案，并计算团簇内外的局域态密度（LDOS）对比。

Result: 环形散射体模型产生了以M点为中心的三重对称椭圆QPI结构，与实验FT-STS测量一致；这些QPI特征在实空间自然生成近静态驻波；LDOS对比的符号和幅度随能量显著变化，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: Au插层于石墨烯空位下方形成的环形散射势能够定量解释实验中观测到的M点附近QPI特征、驻波图案及能量依赖的LDOS对比，为理解金属插层石墨烯体系中的量子干涉现象提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Gold intercalation beneath epitaxial graphene on SiC produces a cluster phase with unusual standing waves and quasiparticle-interference (QPI) features concentrated near the graphene M points. We show that this can be explained by Au intercalation below graphene hollow sites, which induces a local scattering potential on the six surrounding carbon atoms. Within a T-matrix treatment, this ring-like scatterer produces elliptical QPI structures centered near M, in agreement with the experimental FT-STS measurements. We further show that these QPI features naturally generate the nearly stationary standing-wave patterns observed in real space. Finally, we compute the local-density-of-states contrast on and off a small cluster and show that its sign and magnitude are strongly energy dependent, consistent with the experimental observations.

</details>


### [100] [Current-based RF charge sensing in a carbon nanotube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28313)
*Marta Cagetti,Stefan Forstner,Victor Champain,Roger Tormo-Queralt,Christoffer B. Møller,Sergio L. De Bonis,Chandan Samanta,Elsa Vázquez-Rodriguez,Eneko Mateos-Madinabeitia,David A. Czaplewski,Adrian Bachtold*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种在悬浮碳纳米管中实现的电流模式电荷传感器，工作在1.25 MHz的RLC谐振频率下，实现了0.15 μe/√Hz的电荷灵敏度。利用该传感器对同一纳米管中静电定义的双量子点进行了测量，展示了高度规则的电荷稳定图，并在3.56 μs积分时间内实现了单次电荷态读出，信噪比达17，优于现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超灵敏电荷检测技术需要阻抗匹配的谐振电路或毫米级放大器-样品邻近度，这些要求增加了系统复杂性和器件设计的约束。作者旨在引入一种简化方案，同时保持甚至超越现有技术的检测灵敏度。

Method: 采用悬浮碳纳米管中的电流模式电荷传感器，利用RLC槽路在1.25 MHz谐振频率下工作，通过该传感器对同一碳纳米管中静电定义的双量子点进行电荷态测量，并进行单次读出的性能评估。

Result: 实现了0.15 μe/√Hz的电荷灵敏度；在3.56 μs积分时间下完成双量子点电荷态单次读出，在10^7次测量中无错误分配，信噪比达到17，超越现有技术水平。

Conclusion: 该电流模式电荷传感器在保持超高灵敏度的同时简化了系统要求，为量子信息处理和凝聚态物理探测提供了更为灵活的器件设计方案。

Abstract: Ultra-sensitive charge detection is a widely used tool for quantum electronics with applications in quantum information processing and in probing the physics of condensed matter systems. Existing approaches require either an impedance-matched resonant circuit, or millimeter-scale proximity between amplifier and sample, both adding complexity and constraining device design. In this work, we introduce a current-mode charge sensor in a suspended carbon nanotube, operating at the $1.25$ MHz resonance of an RLC tank circuit and achieving a charge sensitivity of $0.15~μe/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. We utilize it to measure a double quantum dot (DQD) electrostatically defined in the same nanotube, revealing a highly regular charge stability diagram. We perform single-shot readout of the DQD charge state at an integration time of $3.56~μ\mathrm{s}$, without any false assignments over $10^{7}$ measurements and a signal-to-noise ratio of 17 exceeding the state of the art.

</details>


### [101] [Anomalous metal and superconducting phases in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28425)
*Anna Okounkova,Abigail Sohm,Tobias Faehndrich,Manish Kumar,Derek Waleffe,Jiaqiang Yan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Joshua Folk,Matthew Yankowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了菱面体石墨烯/WSe2异质结中二维超导的有限电阻饱和现象，发现栅极空间中存在零电阻超导区和有限饱和电阻区（异常金属态），并通过外加面内磁场实现了这两类区域之间的锐利边界转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管石墨烯基系统中已广泛观察到二维超导性，且许多表现出非常规配对的特征，但在多个栅极调控的相中，电阻在低温下不消失而饱和于有限值，这一"异常金属态"现象在薄膜超导中已被观察数十年但缺乏公认的理论解释。本文旨在通过超高洁净度的菱面体石墨烯平台对这一问题进行系统性研究，并利用其可调控性约束外部解释。

Method: 在菱面体石墨烯/WSe2衬底异质结上进行系统的输运测量，扫描栅极空间（位移场与载流子密度参数空间），在零磁场下识别零电阻超导口袋和有限饱和电阻口袋；然后在毫开尔文基温下施加小面内磁场，观察这两类口袋的合并与边界演化，对比其转变行为（温度、垂直磁场、电流的临界值跃迁）。

Result: 在栅极空间中发现孤立的零电阻超导区与有限饱和电阻区并存，两者在从超导态到正常态的突变转变现象学上高度相似（温度、垂直磁场、电流超过临界值时出现突跳）。小面内磁场使这些口袋扩张并合并，在毫开尔文基温下形成零电阻态与有限电阻态之间的锐利边界。有限电阻态再现了与异常金属相关的关键现象学特征。

Conclusion: 超高纯净度的菱面体石墨烯对该现象的高度可调控性和可重复性对外部解释施加了强约束，为理解异常金属态提供了新的实验平台，并表明该有限饱和电阻状态很可能是内禀的量子态而非外因所致。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductivity is now well established in graphene-based systems, with many such realizations showing evidence for unconventional pairing. Yet in several of the gate-tuned phases that otherwise exhibit clear signatures of superconductivity, the resistance does not vanish as temperature is lowered, instead saturating at a finite value. Here we report a systematic study of this behavior in rhombohedral graphene on a WSe$_2$ substrate, finding regions of gate space with zero-resistance superconductivity alongside others with finite saturation resistance. At zero magnetic field, these regions appear as isolated pockets in gate space that otherwise exhibit strikingly similar phenomenology, including abrupt transitions to the normal state as temperature, perpendicular magnetic field, and current are raised above critical values. A small in-plane field expands and merges these pockets without qualitatively altering their behavior, producing a sharp boundary at millikelvin base temperature between states of zero or finite resistance. The finite-resistance state reproduces key phenomenology associated with the anomalous metal, a state that has been observed in thin-film superconductors for decades but lacks an accepted theoretical explanation. The tunability and reproducibility of ultra-clean rhombohedral graphene place strong constraints on extrinsic explanations and provide a new platform for understanding this behavior.

</details>


### [102] [Observation of long-lived spin order in nanoconfined water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28480)
*Rohma Khan,Kang Xu,Nathaniel Jeffries,Ankit Bhardwaj,Boya Radha,Daniela Pagliero,Carlos A. Meriles*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用氮空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）光谱技术探测5.6纳米通道中受限于纳米尺度的水的动力学行为。系统在整个温度范围内保持液态，扩散性受到强烈抑制，可直接检测其1H NMR谱。研究发现质子共振偶发地转变为分裂达数十千赫兹的双峰，被归因于由激光照射期间光注入的溶剂化电子所引发的长寿命顺磁电荷复合物介导的超精细相互作用。该特征的间歇性出现表明受限液体中存在由相互关联的电荷-水合复合物群构成的亚稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度受限液体表现出与体相液体显著不同的行为，但在受控条件下研究其动力学具有实验挑战性。作者旨在利用NV中心NMR技术探索5.6纳米通道中受限水的温度依赖性行为，以克服这一实验困难。

Method: 使用氮空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）光谱技术，对5.6纳米通道中受限的水进行温度依赖的探测，直接测量其1H NMR谱并追踪质子共振的谱线形态变化。

Result: 系统在整个测试温度范围内保持液态且扩散性被强烈抑制，可检测到清晰的1H NMR谱。质子共振偶发地分裂为具有数十千赫兹分裂的双峰，此特征被归因于长寿命顺磁电荷复合物介导的超精细相互作用。该双峰的间歇性出现表明受限液体中形成了电荷-水合复合物的亚稳态关联群体。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了纳米受限水中存在由光注入的溶剂化电子引发的长寿命电荷-水合复合物亚稳态，表明受限几何结构可以稳定体相中不存在的集体电荷关联状态，为探索纳米尺度受限液体的新奇量子行为和动力学提供了新途径。

Abstract: Liquids confined to nanometer-scale geometries exhibit behavior that departs markedly from their bulk counterparts, yet studying their dynamics under controlled conditions remains experimentally challenging. Here, we use nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy to probe water confined in 5.6 nm channels as a function of temperature. The system remains liquid throughout the investigated temperature range and exhibits strongly suppressed diffusivity, enabling direct detection of its 1H NMR spectrum. Occasionally, the proton resonance transforms into a doublet with a splitting of several tens of kilohertz, which we tentatively attribute to hyperfine interactions mediated by long-lived paramagnetic charge complexes, in turn seeded by solvated electrons optically injected during laser illumination. The intermittent appearance of this feature suggests a metastable state comprising a correlated population of charge-hydration complexes extending throughout the confined liquid.

</details>

### [103] [Coordinate and Momentum Distributions of a Small Composite System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27244)
*Dmitry Naplekov,Yury Bezruk,Vladimir Yanovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由可移动外壳和有限数量内部粒子组成的孤立小系统，在总能量和动量守恒条件下，导出了外壳坐标和动量的精确分布。坐标分布由有限分支组成且可能包含平台区域，动量分布对应能量在系统自由度间的非均匀分配。发现坐标分布仅依赖内部粒子数量而不依赖其运动维度，动量分布则依赖自由度数目，因此通过比较二者可推断系统内部结构。此外还分析了由内部粒子碰撞引起的外壳混沌运动，该运动与布朗运动不同，在无外部介质时仍持续存在，并给出了外壳均方根偏差的通用显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解孤立小型系统中可移动外壳与有限数量内部粒子之间的相互作用机制，揭示系统各自由度上能量分配的统计规律，并通过外壳的可观测分布特征推断系统的内部结构。

Method: 在总能量和总动量守恒的约束条件下，对不同类型的内部粒子运动进行精确的解析推导，分别计算外壳坐标和动量的精确分布；通过比较两者的依赖性（内部粒子数目vs自由度数目）来区分坐标分布与动量分布所携带的结构信息；同时对粒子碰撞引起的外壳混沌运动进行统计分析，推导均方根偏差的通用表达式。

Result: 得到了外壳坐标和动量的精确分布，坐标分布包含有限分支及平台区域；坐标分布依赖于内部粒子数但与其运动维度无关，动量分布则依赖于自由度数目；外壳的混沌运动在无外部介质时依然存在（区别于布朗运动）；获得了依赖于内部粒子数量和质量的均方根偏差通用显式表达式。

Conclusion: 外壳的坐标分布和动量分布分别编码了系统内部结构的不同方面（粒子数目vs自由度数目），两者相结合可以推断系统的内部构型；外壳的混沌运动被证明是内部粒子碰撞的固有结果，无需外部介质的驱动，其统计特性可通过通用表达式定量描述。

Abstract: An isolated small system consisting of a movable shell and a finite number of internal particles is considered. Exact distributions of the shell coordinates and momenta are obtained for various types of internal particle motion under the conditions of conservation of the total energy and momentum of the system. The coordinate distribution consists of a finite number of branches and may contain plateau regions. The momentum distribution corresponds to a non-uniform distribution of energy among the degrees of freedom of the system. It is shown that the coordinate distribution depends on the number of internal particles and remains unchanged when the dimensionality of their motion varies, whereas the momentum distribution depends on the number of degrees of freedom. Consequently, comparison of the coordinate and momentum distributions of the shell makes it possible to draw conclusions about the internal structure of the system. The chaotic motion of the shell caused by impacts from the internal particles is analyzed; unlike Brownian motion, it persists even in the absence of an external medium. A universal expression for the root-mean-square deviation of the shell is obtained explicitly, depending on the number of internal particles and their masses.

</details>


### [104] [When trajectory-based bounds fail: information thermodynamics under noisy feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27299)
*Natalia Ruiz-Pino,Ludovic Bellon,Antonio Prados*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文在欠阻尼反馈控制系统中，实验与理论相结合地比较了三种信息论界限（转移熵、不可用信息、马尔可夫互信息）在有测量噪声和非马尔可夫控制序列下的表现，发现没有一种界限是普遍最优的，且轨迹依赖型界限在测量噪声下迅速劣化，导致马尔可夫互信息界限在较广噪声范围内变得更紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息引擎通过反馈从热涨落中提取功，多种基于信息论的界限已被提出，但它们在现实条件下（测量有噪声、反馈具有时间相关性）的相对性能尚不清楚。理解不同信息界限在有噪声环境中的适用性和鲁棒性，对于实际信息热力学系统的描述至关重要。

Method: 在欠阻尼反馈控制系统（马尔可夫测量但非马尔可夫控制序列）中，通过实验与理论分析相结合的方法，比较三种代表性界限：基于转移熵、不可用信息以及马尔可夫互信息的信息界限。系统性地考察测量噪声对各类信息量度的影响，并进行性能交叉分析。

Result: 三种信息界限中没有一种是普遍最优的。测量噪声优先影响依赖详细轨迹统计的信息度量，而对基于瞬时相关性的量度影响较小，导致轨迹依赖型界限快速劣化，出现马尔可夫互信息界限在较广噪声范围内比不可用信息界限更紧的交叉现象。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了依赖详细轨迹统计的信息论描述在现实条件下的普遍局限性，为超越理想化反馈协议的信息热力学提供了统一的视角，对设计真实环境中的信息引擎具有指导意义。

Abstract: Information engines exploit feedback to extract work from thermal fluctuations, extending the second law of thermodynamics through information-theoretic bounds. While several such bounds have been proposed, their relative performance under realistic conditions---where measurements are noisy and feedback is temporally correlated---remains largely unclear. Here, we experimentally and theoretically investigate this problem in an underdamped feedback-controlled system with Markovian measurements but non-Markovian control sequences. We compare three representative bounds derived from transfer entropy, unavailable information, and Markovian mutual information, and find that none is universally optimal. Instead, measurement noise preferentially affects information measures that rely on detailed trajectory statistics, while leaving quantities based on instantaneous correlations comparatively robust. As a consequence, trajectory-dependent bounds deteriorate rapidly, giving rise to a crossover in which the Markovian mutual-information bound becomes tighter than the unavailable-information bound over a broad range of measurement noise. Our results reveal a general limitation of information-theoretic descriptions that rely on detailed trajectory statistics in realistic settings and provide a unified perspective on information thermodynamics beyond idealised feedback protocols.

</details>


### [105] [From pink to brown: Interaction-driven noise color shift in the Aubry-Andr{é} Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27340)
*M. Jiménez-Valdez,S. A. Montes-Camacho,E. J. Torres-Herrera*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了准周期势作用下的一维格点中金属-绝缘体相变，通过功率谱（能级间距傅里叶变换的模平方）来刻画相变。在无相互作用情况下，功率谱在所有区间均表现为棕噪声行为，无法捕捉相变信号；而在有相互作用时，金属相表现为粉噪声，绝缘相为棕噪声，并辅以能级间距分布和能级数方差加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的谱统计量在刻画准周期势系统金属-绝缘体相变时可能存在局限，尤其是无相互作用情形下难以捕捉清晰的相变信号。论文旨在探索功率谱这一工具能否更有效地表征该相变，并检验其在有/无相互作用两种情况下的适用性。

Method: 使用自旋1/2粒子模型，在一维格点中加入在位准周期势和可调最近邻相互作用。定义功率谱为相邻能级间距的傅里叶变换的模平方，通过分析其噪声类型（粉噪声vs棕噪声）来表征相变；同时采用能级间距分布、能级数方差以及能量本征态结构作为补充判据。

Result: 无相互作用时，功率谱在所有参数区间均呈现棕噪声行为，无法清晰分辨金属-绝缘体相变；有相互作用时，金属相呈现粉噪声特征，绝缘相呈现棕噪声特征，功率谱能够有效区分两相。能级间距分布、能级数方差和本征态结构分析为这些结论提供了支撑。

Conclusion: 功率谱作为一种谱统计工具，在有相互作用的情形下能够有效表征一维准周期势系统的金属-绝缘体相变，而在无相互作用时其判别能力有限。本征态结构和谱统计量均能作为该系统中相变的可靠指标。

Abstract: We revisit the metal-insulator transition in a one-dimensional lattice subjected to an on-site quasiperiodic potential, modeled using spin-1/2 particles with tunable nearest-neighbor interactions. To characterize the transition, we examine the power-spectrum, defined as the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of consecutive level spacings. In the absence of interactions, the power-spectrum fails to capture clear signatures of the transition, consistently exhibiting brown-noise-like behavior across regimes. In contrast, the interacting case reveals a marked change: the metallic phase displays spectral fluctuations consistent with pink noise, while the insulating phase is characterized by brown noise. These results are supported by analyses of the level spacing distribution and the level number variance. Additionally, we demonstrate that both statistics and the structure of energy eigenstates provide reliable indicators of the transition, in both interacting and non-interacting scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Chaos and Diffusive Transport from Geometric Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28579)
*Bibek Saha,Abhishek Dhar,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明了几何随机性可以独立地产生量子混沌和扩散输运现象。通过研究具有均匀耦合的随机局部树状分层图上的无相互作用量子粒子，发现层尺寸决定了系统是否出现混沌动力学：广泛的层尺寸产生稳健的量子混沌、能级排斥和扩散输运；而在准一维极限下，局域态和扩展态共存，导致能级排斥被抑制且弹道输运占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌和扩散输运通常在具有微观无序或强多体相互作用的系统中研究。本文旨在探索这些现象能否纯粹由几何随机性产生，建立一种独立于传统无序和多体机制的量子混沌生成途径。

Method: 在随机局部树状分层图（规则方格子或梯子的结构无序推广，等价于具有随机链接的多分量一维链）上研究无相互作用的量子粒子。通过调节层尺寸，系统在较高维度行为（广泛层尺寸）与准一维极限之间过渡，分别分析能级统计（能级排斥）和输运性质（扩散vs弹道）。

Result: 广泛层尺寸导致稳健的量子混沌、能级排斥和扩散输运；准一维极限下，存在大量局域态与扩展态共存，能级排斥被抑制，扩展态驱动弹道输运。

Conclusion: 几何随机性是产生和调控量子混沌的一种基本且独立的机制，为量子混沌研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The physics of quantum chaos and diffusive transport is typically studied in settings with microscopic disorder or many-body interactions. In this Letter, we demonstrate that these phenomena can arise purely from geometric randomness. By studying non-interacting quantum particles on random locally tree-like layered graphs with uniform couplings, we show that the geometric randomness and effective graph dimensionality dictates the presence of chaotic dynamics or lack thereof. These graphs can be considered as structurally disordered generalisations of regular square lattices or ladders, or equivalently as multi-component one-dimensional chains with random links between the components. We find that an extensive layer size yields robust quantum chaos, level repulsion, and diffusive transport. Conversely, in the quasi-one-dimensional limit, we find the coexistence of extensive number of localised and delocalised states -- this leads to suppressed level repulsion accompanied by the latter driving ballistic transport. These results establish geometric randomness as a fundamental and independent mechanism for generating and tuning quantum chaos.

</details>


### [107] [Non-reciprocity drives a Brownian dimer out of equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27740)
*Suman Pramanik,Soham Dutta,Arnab Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了由两个过阻尼单体组成的二维布朗二聚体最小模型，它们被困在各向同性谐振势中，并通过违反牛顿作用-反作用原理的非互易谐振弹簧相互耦合。研究表明，仅凭非互易相互作用就能在无外部时间驱动、仅接触单一热浴的情况下将系统驱动至远离平衡态。在弹簧零静止长度极限下，精确计算了稳态概率分布和电流，该模型映射到布朗回旋这一非平衡现象；对于有限静止长度的弹簧，则通过数值方法计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非互易相互作用作为一种内在驱动力，能否在没有任何外部时间依赖驱动且仅与单一热浴接触的条件下，将系统驱动至远离平衡态。这种非互易耦合违反了经典力学中的作用-反作用原理，可能为理解活性物质和生物系统中的非平衡行为提供新的视角。

Method: 构建二维过阻尼布朗二聚体模型：两个单体位于各向同性谐振陷阱中，由非互易线性弹簧耦合。在零静止长度极限下解析求解Fokker-Planck方程，精确得到稳态概率分布和概率电流；对于有限静止长度的情况，采用数值方法求解。

Result: 非互易相互作用本身即足以将系统驱动至远离平衡态，无需外部时间驱动或多热浴条件。零静止长度极限下的精确解表明系统等价于布朗回旋现象，即粒子围绕共同质心的持续旋转运动，产生非零稳态概率电流。

Conclusion: 非互易弹性耦合可以作为一种纯内在的机制诱导稳态非平衡态，打破了传统上认为需要外部驱动或多温度浴才能维持非平衡状态的认知。该模型提供了一个研究非互易相互作用与布朗运动耦合产生持续回旋流的简洁理论框架。

Abstract: We consider the minimal model of a two dimensional Brownian dimer consisting of two overdamped monomers, trapped in an isotropic harmonic potential and mutually coupled by a non-reciprocal harmonic spring that violates Newton's action-reaction principle. We have shown that the non-reciprocal interaction alone can drive the system far from equilibrium, in the absence of any external time dependent drive and being in contact with a single thermal bath. The exact steady state probability distribution and current are explicitly calculated for the zero-rest-length limit of the spring, which eventually maps our model to another non-equilibrium phenomenon, called Brownian gyration. For a spring with finite rest length, these quantities are calculated numerically.

</details>


### [108] [Ensemble theory of thermodiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27839)
*R. Belousov,J. Elliott,A. Erzberger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用路径系综理论，将耦合的热扩散输运过程建模为图上的动力学过程。仅通过单一平衡模拟校准，该模型即可准确预测硬核气体在广泛温度梯度下的非平衡质量分布，并可通过解析表达式估算Soret系数等输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度梯度驱动的热扩散（Soret效应）会产生物质梯度，这一现象在硬核气体等体系的原子模拟中已被观察到。然而，如何从理论上有效表征这种耦合的热与扩散输运过程，并用极少的数据准确预测非平衡行为，是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 采用路径系综理论（path-ensemble theory），将耦合的热输运和扩散输运过程表示为图（graph）上的动力学。模型参数仅通过单一平衡模拟进行校准，无需依赖非平衡数据。基于校准后的模型，利用解析表达式估算Soret系数等输运性质。

Result: 该模型能够在广泛的应用温度梯度范围内准确预测硬核气体的非平衡质量分布，同时能够通过解析公式估算输运性质（如Soret系数）。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一种高效且数据驱动的方法：仅需一次平衡模拟，即可构建预测非平衡热扩散行为的物理模型，为理解和量化Soret效应提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Heat flow sustained by a temperature difference generates a gradient of matter as observed, for example, in atomistic simulations of hard-core gases. We use path-ensemble theory to represent the coupled thermal and diffusive transport by dynamics on a graph. Calibrated from a single equilibrium simulation, this model accurately predicts nonequilibrium mass distribution in a hard-core gas across a wide range of applied temperature gradients. It also enables estimation of transport properties, such as the Soret coefficient, using analytical expressions.

</details>


### [109] [Mpemba effect in a chemomechanical model of the Kinesin molecular motor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27998)
*Karthik Cheruvary,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在驱动蛋白（Kinesin）六态化学机械网络模型中研究了Mpemba效应（远离平衡的系统反而更快弛豫）。研究发现该效应在化学平衡下存在，其定性特征可由自由能景观理解；机械和化学驱动主要通过重塑Mpemba相图来影响该效应，而不改变其弛豫现象学；驱动蛋白速度的弛豫与底层随机动力学的异常弛豫一致，提供了实验上可观测的Mpemba效应标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应已在多种物理系统中被广泛研究，但在生物学相关的非平衡过程中的作用仍基本未被探索。本文旨在将Mpemba效应的研究拓展到生物分子马达这一生命系统中的非平衡化学机械网络。

Method: 采用驱动蛋白（Kinesin）的六态化学机械网络模型，分别在平衡和非平衡条件下研究异常弛豫。首先在化学平衡下建立Mpemba效应的存在性并从自由能景观角度理解其定性特征；随后通过引入机械和化学驱动力考察细致平衡破缺的影响；最后分析驱动蛋白速度弛豫与底层随机动力学异常弛豫之间的对应关系。

Result: （1）在化学平衡下确认了Mpemba效应的存在；（2）打破细致平衡主要重塑Mpemba相图，但在物理相关参数范围内不从根本上改变弛豫现象学；（3）驱动蛋白速度的弛豫镜像了底层随机动力学的异常弛豫，提供了实验可访问的Mpemba效应实验签名。

Conclusion: 分子马达有望成为研究生命系统中异常弛豫的基线系统，并为在非平衡生化网络中探索Mpemba效应提供了更广泛的框架。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, wherein a system prepared farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, has been extensively investigated in a wide range of physical systems. In contrast, its role in biologically relevant non-equilibrium processes remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate anomalous relaxation in the six-state chemomechanical network model of the Kinesin molecular motor under both equilibrium and non-equilibrium conditions. We first establish the existence of the Mpemba effect in chemical equilibrium and show that many of its qualitative features can be understood from the underlying free-energy landscape. We then examine the effects of mechanical and chemical driving, showing that breaking detailed balance primarily reshapes the Mpemba phase diagram without qualitatively altering the relaxation phenomenology over the physically relevant parameter regime. Finally, we demonstrate that the relaxation of the motor velocity also mirrors the anomalous relaxation of the underlying stochastic dynamics, thereby identifying an experimentally accessible signature of the Mpemba effect. Our results establish molecular motors as a promising baseline for studying anomalous relaxation in living systems and suggest a broader framework for exploring the Mpemba effect in non-equilibrium biochemical networks.

</details>


### [110] [Three-Dimensional Kardar--Parisi--Zhang Scaling in Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28106)
*Junhui Cao,Denis Novokreschenov,Artem Alexandrov,Timothy Halpin-Healy,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出三维激子-极化激元晶体作为观测三维KPZ（Kardar-Parisi-Zhang）普适类的平台。通过从随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，消除密度和库激模，得到3+1维KPZ方程。数值模拟显示其一阶相干性指数与3D KPZ基准一致（β=0.1845, χ=0.3135），为高维非平衡普适性提供了可控的量子流体途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ普适性是由微观细致平衡破缺产生的宏观标度行为的重要范例，但迄今仅在二维系统中（如驱动凝聚体和生长界面）得到验证。在三维空间维度中演示KPZ标度仍然是一个重大挑战，因此需要寻找合适的平台来实现三维KPZ普适性的观测。

Method: 从三维光子晶体下极化激元带中凝聚体满足的随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，通过绝热消除大质量密度模和库激模，推导出有效3+1维KPZ方程描述凝聚体相位。随后对KPZ方程和完整的驱动-耗散极化激元模型进行数值模拟，分析一阶相干函数的时间与空间标度行为。

Result: 数值模拟显示存在中间渐近区间，其中一阶相干性满足-ln|g1(0,Δt)|∝|Δt|^{2β}和-ln|g1(Δr,0)|∝|Δr|^{2χ}的标度律，指数β=0.1845和χ=0.3135与3+1维KPZ基准一致。

Conclusion: 三维极化激元晶体可作为可控的量子流体平台，为实现高维非平衡普适类（即3D KPZ普适性）的观测提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality provides an example of macroscopic scaling generated by microscopic violation of detailed balance. While one- and two-dimensional realizations have been explored in driven condensates and growing interfaces, demonstrating KPZ scaling in three spatial dimensions remains a major challenge. Here we propose a three-dimensional exciton-polariton crystal as a platform for observation of 3D KPZ universality. Starting from a stochastic driven-dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a condensate formed in a three-dimensional photonic-crystal lower-polariton band, we eliminate the massive density and reservoir modes and obtain an effective $3+1$-dimensional KPZ equation for the condensate phase. Numerical simulations of both the KPZ equation and the full driven-dissipative polariton model show an intermediate-asymptotic regime in which the first-order coherence obeys $-\ln |\gone(0,Δt)|\propto |Δt|^{2β}$ and $-\ln |\gone(Δr,0)|\propto |Δr|^{2χ}$, with exponents consistent with the $3+1$ KPZ benchmarks $β= 0.1845$, $χ= 0.3135$. Our results identify three-dimensional polariton crystals as a controllable quantum fluid route to higher-dimensional nonequilibrium universality.

</details>


### [111] [From Exponential to Gaussian Tails: Fractal Wavefront Scaling and the $κ$-Weibull Distribution at Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28172)
*O. V. Pavlovsky,M. Yu. Satleikin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过元胞自动机模型研究活性介质中的临界演化行为，揭示了两种截然不同的动力学状态（衰逝活动和全填充活动波），并分析了临界区间内的分形维数与涨落统计的桥接特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性介质中临界演化（critical evolution）的本质特征，探索两种定性不同动力学状态之间的转变机制及其统计特性。

Method: 采用元胞自动机（cellular automaton）对活性介质进行建模，系统依据随机规则演化。通过分析分形维数（fractal dimension）和涨落统计（fluctuation statistics）来刻画不同动力学状态，并识别临界区间内的雪崩（avalanche）驱动行为。

Result: 发现系统存在两个定性不同的动力学状态：衰逝活动态和全填充活动波态，各自具有独立的分形维数和涨落统计特征。临界区间内系统展现出非平凡的分形维数和桥接两种状态的涨落分布，该桥接行为源于雪崩动力学的主导作用。

Conclusion: 临界区间的桥接特征由雪崩驱动机制所致，这为理解临界演化中不同动力学状态间的连续过渡提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The paper examines the features of critical evolution in an active medium modeled by a cellular automaton. The system evolves according to stochastic rules, exhibiting two qualitatively distinct dynamical regimes: one of fading activity and another of all-filling activity waves. Each regime is characterized by its own fractal dimension and fluctuation statistics, both of which are analyzed in detail. Within the critical interval, the system displays a nontrivial fractal dimension and a fluctuation distribution that bridges the two regimes. This bridging behavior arises because, in the critical interval, the dynamics are governed by avalanches.

</details>


### [112] [Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28255)
*Denis Bernard,Friedrich Hübner,Stefano Scopa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并求解了相互作用量子对称排他过程（IQSEP）这一模型族，描述了带电粒子在晶格边上进行随机量子跳跃的动力学，其中跳跃振幅依赖于邻近位点的占据数。该模型在无相互作用时退化为标准量子简单对称排他过程（展现相干扩散输运），在有相互作用时捕获非相干扩散输运及其涨落。通过适当缩放相互作用强度，定义了保留有限相干长度的介观标度区间，该区间在小尺度上呈相干行为、在大尺度上呈非相干行为，其标度理论超越了标准涨落流体力学的范畴，解释了相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个统一的框架来描述相互作用对量子扩散输运中相干性-非相干性转变的影响，以及量子相干性在介观尺度上的涨落行为。标准的涨落流体力学无法捕捉相互作用扩散系统中的量子相干效应。

Method: 引入IQSEP模型族，利用依赖邻近位点占据数的跳跃振幅来刻画粒子间相互作用；在三种标度场景下（无相互作用、相互作用量级为1、相互作用随晶格间距适当缩放）分别分析模型行为，建立介观标度区间以保留有限相干长度。

Result: 在无相互作用极限下恢复标准QSESP的相干扩散输运；在相互作用为1量级时得到密度依赖的扩散率和迁移率，与宏观涨落理论一致；通过缩放相互作用强度，证明了存在一个介于相干与非相干行为之间的介观标度区间，并导出了超越标准涨落流体力学的新标度理论。

Conclusion: IQSEP提供了一种统一的介观标度框架，能够解释相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落，是对标准涨落流体力学的重要扩展。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process (IQSEP), a family of models describing the stochastic quantum hopping of charged particles along the edges of a lattice, with hopping amplitudes that depend on the occupations of neighbouring sites. In the absence of interactions, they reduce to the standard quantum simple symmetric exclusion process, exhibiting coherent diffusive transport. For interactions of order one, they capture incoherent diffusive transport and its fluctuations, characterized by density-dependent diffusivity and mobility, making contact with the macroscopic fluctuation theory. By rescaling the interaction strength appropriately with the lattice mesh, we define a mesoscopic scaling regime that retains a finite coherence length in the continuous thermodynamic limit. This regime interpolates between coherent behavior at small length scales and incoherent behavior at large scales. The resulting scaling theory accounts for fluctuations of quantum coherences in interacting diffusive systems, going beyond the scope of standard fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Synchronization, Kinematic Waves and Spike-Phase-Separation in Feedback Ising Neural Networks on Heterogeneous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28275)
*Anna Poggialini,Irem Topal,Fabrizio Lombardi,Daniele De Martino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由稳态反馈回路驱动的动力学Ising神经网络，利用Curie-Weiss异质平均场理论，解析地刻画了异质网络上通过Andronov-Hopf分岔发生的宏观同步极限环，并证明网络异质性通过度矩比率显式控制振荡的涌现，还发现度异质性解耦了神经元尖峰率与突触尖峰率，产生了同质系统中不存在的物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构异质性约束复杂系统中的集体动力学，但非平衡态下其解析可处理性仍然有限。作者旨在突破这一限制，为非平衡神经网络的异质性效应提供可解析描述的数学框架。

Method: 采用Curie-Weiss异质平均场近似（由Monte Carlo模拟验证），研究由稳态反馈回路驱动的动力学Ising神经网络，通过度矩比率解析推导相边界，并分析Andronov-Hopf分岔和pitchfork分岔导致的动力学转变。

Result: 度异质性使每个神经元的尖峰率m与每个突触的尖峰率u解耦，产生同质系统中不存在的现象：(i)从网络边缘到枢纽的按度排序的传播运动波；(ii)通过pitchfork分岔产生的低温相分离态。对于高度异质拓扑，相分离不动点可稳定化并动态破坏同步极限环。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解异质性如何调节神经网络的宏观振荡和非平衡相变提供了完整的数学框架，揭示了网络结构与动力学涌现行为之间的深层关联。

Abstract: Structural heterogeneity constrains collective dynamics in complex systems. However, its analytical tractability out of equilibrium remains limited. In this work, we study a class of kinetic Ising neural networks driven out of equilibrium by a homeostatic feedback loop between the neuronal excitability and the population firing rate. Using a Curie-Weiss heterogeneous mean-field approximation validated by Monte Carlo simulations, we provide an analytical characterization of how a macroscopic synchronized limit cycle emerges via an Andronov-Hopf bifurcation on heterogeneous networks. We derive closed-form phase boundaries and show that the onset of oscillations is explicitly controlled by network heterogeneity through the degree moment ratio. Degree heterogeneity decouples the spiking rate per neuron m from the spiking rate per synapse u, generating physical phenomena absent in homogeneous systems. These include (i) kinematic waves of sequential, degree-ordered activations propagating from the network periphery to the hubs, and (ii) a low-temperature phase-separated state emerging via a pitchfork bifurcation. We prove that for highly heterogeneous topologies, this phase-separated fixed point stabilizes and dynamically destroys the synchronized limit cycle. These results provide a mathematical framework for understanding how heterogeneity regulates macroscopic oscillations and out-of-equilibrium transitions in neural networks

</details>


### [114] [Convergence to the exact kinetics of the one-dimensional Riviera model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28419)
*Pascal Viot*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对Riviera模型（一种随机顺序沉积模型），提出了一种可在任意有限阶实现的系统化闭合方案来求解其动力学方程，并通过逐阶求解展示了该方法快速收敛到精确行为，在高阶下能极其精确地估计堵塞密度，同时保留完整时间演化的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riviera模型虽然规则简单（一维中当目标位点为孤立空位时沉积被拒），但至今没有精确解析解。作者旨在发展一种系统性的方法来解决这一长期未解的问题。

Method: 推导出控制该模型动力学的无穷级动力学方程层级，引入一种可在任意有限阶实现的系统化闭合方案，并逐阶求解所得方程组。

Result: 逐阶求解显示所得动力学快速收敛至精确行为；在高阶下，该方法给出了极其精确的堵塞密度估计，同时保留了完整时间依赖演化的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该闭合方案为Riviera模型提供了有效的近似解析求解路径，兼顾高精度与解析可表达性，是该模型理论分析的重要进展。

Abstract: The Riviera model is a random sequential deposition model for house building on a lattice, in which a house cannot be built when both nearest-neighbor sites are already occupied. In one dimension, an attempted deposition is therefore rejected whenever the target site is an isolated vacancy. Despite the apparent simplicity of this rule, no exact analytical solution of the original model is currently known. We derive an infinite hierarchy of kinetic equations governing its dynamics and introduce a systematic closure scheme that can be implemented at arbitrary finite order. Solving the resulting systems order by order, we show that the corresponding kinetics converges rapidly toward the exact behavior. At high order, the method yields an exceptionally accurate estimate of the jamming density while retaining explicit analytical expressions for the full time-dependent evolution.

</details>

### [115] [Bose-Einstein condensation and superfluidity on a fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27470)
*Vira Shyta,Flavio S. Nogueira,Ashley M. Cook*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对易（模糊）球面上的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）和超流现象。在二维紧致空间中，非对易性会增强有序相，提升BEC临界温度，并改变超流密度的温度依赖关系——在大球极限下，正常流体分数呈线性温度依赖（类似Uemura定律），而非普通球面上的立方依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据Hohenberg定理，二维空间中温度T>0时玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚不可能发生，而二维超流却可以在有限温度下存在（通过破坏伽利略不变性）。作者希望在紧致二维空间的非对易框架下（模糊球面）重新审视BEC和超流性，探索非对易性如何影响量子相变和有序相，并寻找与高温超导实验现象（Uemura定律）的可能联系。

Method: 将标量玻色场提升为N×N矩阵，定义在非对易（模糊）球面上，其中维度N与非对易参数相关。分别分析理想和弱相互作用玻色气体在模糊球面上的BEC临界温度，并与普通交换球面S^2进行对比。同时研究弱相互作用玻色系统的超流响应，扩展涡旋缺陷的定义到本质上非对易的球面，并计算大球极限下的超流密度。

Result: 非对易性有利于有序相的形成，增强BEC和超流。在每种情况下，模糊球面上的BEC临界温度均高于普通交换球面S^2。超流密度计算表明，在大球极限下，模糊球面的正常流体分数呈线性温度依赖（∼T），而交换S^2上呈二维立方依赖（∼T^3）。这种源自非对易性的线性依赖让人联想到铜氧化物高温超导体中的Uemura定律。

Conclusion: 非对易空间为BEC和超流提供了新的物理平台。模糊球面具有与平面不同的热力学极限（这一点不同于普通球面），其非对易效应导致观测量（如超流密度）出现独特的温度依赖关系。这种线性温度依赖与高温超导体的Uemura定律相似，暗示非对易几何可能在量子凝聚现象中发挥重要作用，并有望在实验中观测到。

Abstract: According to Hohenberg's theorem, Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) in two dimensions is impossible for any temperature $T>0$. By contrast, superfluidity does occur in two dimensions at finite temperatures; it emerges due to the breaking of Galilei invariance. Here we consider BEC and superfluidity on a compact two-dimensional space taking the form of a non-commutative ("fuzzy") sphere, where the scalar bosonic fields are promoted to $N\times N$ matrices. The dimension $N$ is related to the non-commutativity parameter of space and introduces an additional scale into the system. We find that non-commutativity favors ordered phases and so enhances BEC and superfluidity. We analyze BEC in ideal and weakly interacting Bose gases on a fuzzy sphere, finding in each case that the critical temperature of BEC is greater compared to that found in the case of a commutative sphere $S^2$. Then we investigate the superfluid response of weakly interacting Bose systems. To account for vortices in a superfluid, we show that, even on an ordinary sphere, the collective coordinates of vortices induce non-commutativity. With this in mind, we extend the definition of vortex defects to an inherently non-commutative sphere studied here, where the notion of a point is untenable. The non-commutativity is expected to be experimentally relevant to BEC and superfluidity since the fuzzy sphere has a thermodynamic limit distinct from the one defined over a plane, unlike the $S^2$ case. The significance of this difference is illustrated by the superfluid density calculation indicating that, in the large sphere limit, the normal fluid fraction on the fuzzy sphere yields a linear in $T$ dependence, while on a commutative $S^2$ it exhibits the usual two-dimensional $\sim T^3$ behavior. This linear dependence, arising directly from non-commutativity, is reminiscent of Uemura's law in cuprate high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>


### [116] [Observation of Moiré Time Crystal in Floquet-driven Rydberg Atomic Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27999)
*Shuai Shi,Dong-Yang Zhu,Yu Yang,Chu-Rong Pan,Jing-Wen Tang,Ya-Peng Zhang,Yan-Li Zhou,Wei-Tao Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了莫尔时间晶体——由双色驱动场中长程里德堡相互作用与耗散共同作用产生的拍频梳状莫尔图样，具有超长拍频周期，并绘制了系统的相图，确认了其对激光失谐扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔时间晶体作为非平衡量子相，源于两个不同频率（至少一个为时间晶体的本征对称破缺振荡）的相干干涉，其标志是超长拍频周期，是空间莫尔条纹在时间域的映射。然而至今尚无实验实现，因此作者旨在首次实现这种莫尔时间晶体。

Method: 利用双色驱动场施加两个不同频率的驱动，通过长程里德堡相互作用与耗散之间的协同效应，产生梳状莫尔图样（拍频梳），将亚谐波周期性与基频叠加。通过改变其中一个驱动频率，观察莫尔图样在频谱上的交错移动；并实验绘制系统相图以表征莫尔时间有序区域。

Result: 实验上成功观测到梳状莫尔图样，映射出由两个失配驱动形成的莫尔拍频模式；系统相图显示存在一个鲁棒区域，其中莫尔时间有序对激光失谐扰动具有持久性。

Conclusion: 该莫尔时间晶体为探索涌现的慢-快动力学和合成时空对称性提供了可控平台，并为在驱动量子多体系统中工程化复杂时间有序性开辟了新途径。

Abstract: A Moiré time crystal is a non-equilibrium quantum phase emerging from the coherent interference of two distinct frequencies, at least one being the intrinsic oscillation of a symmetry-broken time crystal. Its hallmark is an ultra-long beat period, reflecting a time-domain mapping of the Moiré fringes that arise from mismatched spatial lattices. However, to date, no experimental realization of such a Moiré time crystal has been reported. In this work, by applying a bichromatic driving field with two distinct frequencies, we demonstrate that the interplay between long-range Rydberg interactions and dissipation gives rise to a unique comb-like Moiré pattern characterized by a beat-note comb, which superimposes subharmonic periodicity and fundamental frequencies. This Moiré pattern formed by two mismatched drives is staggered in the spectrum as the frequency of one driver changes. We experimentally map the phase diagram of the system and identify a robust region where the Moiré temporal order persists against perturbations in laser detuning. The reported Moiré time crystal not only provides a controllable platform for exploring emergent slow-fast dynamics and synthetic space-time symmetries but also opens avenues for engineering complex temporal order in driven quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [117] [FFLO transition and quantum criticality in polarized Fermi gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28335)
*Francesco Pirolo,Leonardo Pisani,Pierbiagio Pieri*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过图解t-matrix方法研究了二维费米气体中从极化正常相到FFLO态的零温相变，发现标准非自洽理论因严重违反Luttinger定理而产生非物理的相图，进而引入最小自洽扩展来恢复Luttinger定理并得到物理自洽的相图，随后表征了FFLO量子临界行为，并比较了二维与三维系统的临界性质，最后在三维情形下证明了顶点修正无关紧要，将FFLO量子相变置于平均场普适类中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准非自洽的t-matrix理论在描述二维费米气体从极化正常相到FFLO态的转变时产生非物理的相图，其根源在于严重违反Luttinger定理。因此需要寻找一种既能保持原形式解析简洁性，又能恢复Luttinger定理一致性的改进方案，以获得物理上可信的相图并进一步研究该量子临界行为的普适类。

Method: 采用图解t-matrix方法；首先指认标准非自洽理论的缺陷（违反Luttinger定理），然后引入最小自洽扩展方案以恢复Luttinger定理；通过准粒子衰减率、准粒子权重、动量分布以及费米子和玻色子自由度的临界动力学来表征FFLO量子临界行为；对比二维与三维系统，基于多数与少数费米面嵌套的统一几何图像理解临界性质；在三维情形下利用Hertz-Millis框架分析顶点修正的相关性。

Result: （1）标准非自洽理论因严重违反Luttinger定理而产生非物理相图；（2）所引入的最小自洽扩展在很大程度上恢复了对Luttinger定理的符合，并在整个相互作用范围内给出物理一致的相图；（3）基于该改进描述，全面表征了FFLO转变的量子临界行为（准粒子衰减率、准粒子权重、动量分布、临界动力学）；（4）二维与三维系统的临界性质可在多数与少数费米面嵌套的统一几何图像下理解；（5）三维情形下，Hertz-Millis框架表明顶点修正是无关（不可约相关的），FFLO量子相变属于平均场普适类，类似于巡游反铁磁体。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在t-matrix框架下恢复Luttinger定理对获得物理一致的FFLO相图至关重要，并在改进后给出了全面的量子临界表征；二维与三维系统的临界行为可用费米面嵌套的几何图像统一理解；三维FFLO量子相变属于平均场普适类（顶点修正无关），与巡游反铁磁体中的量子临界行为高度类似。

Abstract: We investigate the zero-temperature transition from the polarized normal phase to the FFLO state in a two-dimensional Fermi gas by means of a diagrammatic t-matrix approach. We first show that the standard non-self-consistent theory produces an unphysical phase diagram because of a severe violation of the Luttinger theorem. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a minimal self- consistent extension that largely restores compliance with the Luttinger theorem while preserving the analytical simplicity of the original formalism. This leads to a physically consistent phase diagram over the whole interaction range. Building on this improved description, we characterize the quantum critical behavior of the FFLO transition through the quasiparticle decay rates, quasiparticle weights, momentum distributions, and the critical dynamics of both fermionic and bosonic degrees of freedom. We further compare the two- and three-dimensional systems, showing that their critical properties can be understood within a unified geometrical picture based on the nesting of the majority and minority Fermi surfaces. Finally, for the three-dimensional case, we demonstrate within the Hertz-Millis framework that vertex corrections are irrelevant, thereby placing the FFLO quantum phase transition in the mean-field universality class, in close analogy with itinerant antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [118] [Hubbard physics with ultracold polar molecules: on-site interaction energies for shielded molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28533)
*Carlin Stewart-Wiese,Joy Dutta,Jeremy M. Hutson*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了微波屏蔽超冷分子对在同一晶格位点上的在位相互作用能U。使用具有偶极-偶极长程特征和大排斥核的二维有效势，发现分子间的强运动关联导致U的行为与超冷原子截然不同：在弱晶格下U可为负值，但随着晶格强度增大可穿越零点变为正值。这种行为同时存在于未束缚对和双体束缚态中，后者将开辟具有多位占据的强偶极Hubbard物理新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子作为量子模拟和量子计算平台具有巨大潜力，但其强偶极相互作用和短程排斥核导致的强关联效应尚未被充分理解。特别是同一晶格位点上分子对的相互作用能U是Hubbard模型的核心参数，理解其变化规律对于设计新的量子模拟实验至关重要。

Method: 采用适用于微波屏蔽的二维有效势，该势在长程具有偶极-偶极特征，但在分子靠近时具有非常大的排斥核。在此势下求解同一晶格位点上两个分子的量子运动，研究其相互作用能U随晶格强度的变化，分别考察未束缚对和双体束缚态。

Result: 发现与超冷原子截然不同的行为：某些态的U在弱晶格下为负值，随晶格强度增加可穿越零点变为正值。该现象对未束缚分子对和双体束缚态均成立。双体束缚态的存在将使多位占据的强偶极Hubbard物理成为可能。

Conclusion: 微波屏蔽超冷分子体系中的在位相互作用能U具有非平凡且可控的符号改变行为，这为探索此前未涉及的强偶极Hubbard物理提供了新途径，特别是在多位占据的晶格体系中。

Abstract: We explore on-site interaction energies $U$ for pairs of shielded ultracold molecules on the same lattice site. We use 2-dimensional effective potentials appropriate for microwave shielding, which have dipole-dipole character at long range but feature a very large repulsive core when the two molecules come close together. This causes very strong correlation between the motions of two molecules on the same site. We find behavior very different from that for ultracold atoms: in particular, there are states for which $U$ is negative for weak lattices but crosses zero to positive values as the lattice strength increases. This behavior is found for both unbound pairs and 2-body bound states. The latter will give access to previously unexplored types of strongly dipolar Hubbard physics with multiple site occupancy.

</details>
